cd /app && cat > src/error-options.ts <<'EOF'
export type ErrorStackMode = 'off' | 'string' | 'frames';
export type StripInternalFrames =
  | 'none'
  | 'node'
  | 'superjson'
  | 'node_and_superjson';
export type RedactPaths = 'none' | 'basename' | 'strip_cwd';
export type IncludeCauses = 'none' | 'direct' | 'deep';

export interface ErrorStackOptions {
  mode?: ErrorStackMode;
  normalizeNewlines?: boolean;
  trimLeadingWhitespace?: boolean;
  maxStackLines?: number;
  stripInternalFrames?: StripInternalFrames;
  redactPaths?: RedactPaths;
  includeCauses?: IncludeCauses;
  maxCauseDepth?: number;
  sanitizeMessage?: boolean;
  classFilter?: string | string[];
}

export interface NormalizedErrorStackOptions {
  mode: ErrorStackMode;
  normalizeNewlines: boolean;
  trimLeadingWhitespace: boolean;
  maxStackLines: number | undefined;
  stripInternalFrames: StripInternalFrames;
  redactPaths: RedactPaths;
  includeCauses: IncludeCauses;
  maxCauseDepth: number;
  sanitizeMessage: boolean;
  classFilter: string[];
}

const MODES = new Set<string>(['off', 'string', 'frames']);
const STRIP_MODES = new Set<string>([
  'none',
  'node',
  'superjson',
  'node_and_superjson',
]);
const REDACT_MODES = new Set<string>(['none', 'basename', 'strip_cwd']);
const INCLUDE_CAUSES_MODES = new Set<string>(['none', 'direct', 'deep']);

export function normalizeErrorStackOptions(
  options: unknown
): NormalizedErrorStackOptions | undefined {
  if (
    typeof options !== 'object' ||
    options === null ||
    Array.isArray(options)
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const raw = options as Record<string, unknown>;

  let mode: ErrorStackMode = 'off';
  if (typeof raw.mode === 'string' && MODES.has(raw.mode)) {
    mode = raw.mode as ErrorStackMode;
  }

  let maxStackLines: number | undefined;
  if (raw.maxStackLines !== undefined) {
    if (
      typeof raw.maxStackLines !== 'number' ||
      !Number.isInteger(raw.maxStackLines) ||
      raw.maxStackLines <= 0
    ) {
      // zero, negative, or non-integer values make the config behave like mode=off
      mode = 'off';
    } else {
      maxStackLines = raw.maxStackLines as number;
    }
  }

  let includeCauses: IncludeCauses = 'none';
  if (
    typeof raw.includeCauses === 'string' &&
    INCLUDE_CAUSES_MODES.has(raw.includeCauses)
  ) {
    includeCauses = raw.includeCauses as IncludeCauses;
  }

  let maxCauseDepth = 16;
  if (raw.maxCauseDepth !== undefined) {
    if (
      typeof raw.maxCauseDepth !== 'number' ||
      !Number.isInteger(raw.maxCauseDepth)
    ) {
      includeCauses = 'none';
    } else {
      maxCauseDepth = raw.maxCauseDepth as number;
    }
  }

  let stripInternalFrames: StripInternalFrames = 'none';
  if (
    typeof raw.stripInternalFrames === 'string' &&
    STRIP_MODES.has(raw.stripInternalFrames)
  ) {
    stripInternalFrames = raw.stripInternalFrames as StripInternalFrames;
  }

  let redactPaths: RedactPaths = 'none';
  if (
    typeof raw.redactPaths === 'string' &&
    REDACT_MODES.has(raw.redactPaths)
  ) {
    redactPaths = raw.redactPaths as RedactPaths;
  }

  let classFilter: string[] = [];
  if (Array.isArray(raw.classFilter)) {
    classFilter = raw.classFilter.filter(
      (value): value is string => typeof value === 'string'
    );
  } else if (typeof raw.classFilter === 'string') {
    classFilter = [raw.classFilter];
  }

  return Object.freeze({
    mode,
    normalizeNewlines:
      typeof raw.normalizeNewlines === 'boolean'
        ? raw.normalizeNewlines
        : false,
    trimLeadingWhitespace:
      typeof raw.trimLeadingWhitespace === 'boolean'
        ? raw.trimLeadingWhitespace
        : true,
    maxStackLines,
    stripInternalFrames,
    redactPaths,
    includeCauses,
    maxCauseDepth,
    sanitizeMessage:
      typeof raw.sanitizeMessage === 'boolean' ? raw.sanitizeMessage : false,
    classFilter,
  });
}

const URL_REGEX = /https?:\/\/\S+/g;
const EMAIL_REGEX = /\b[\w.+-]+@[\w-]+\.[\w.-]+\b/g;
const IPV4_REGEX = /\b\d{1,3}(?:\.\d{1,3}){3}\b/g;

export function sanitizeMessage(message: string): string {
  return message
    .replace(URL_REGEX, '[redacted]')
    .replace(EMAIL_REGEX, '[redacted]')
    .replace(IPV4_REGEX, '[redacted]');
}

export class ErrorClassRegistry {

  register(name: string, fn: ErrorStackProcessor): void {
    this.processors.set(name, fn);

  has(name: string): boolean {
    return this.processors.has(name);
  }

  getProcessor(name: string): ErrorStackProcessor | undefined {
    return this.processors.get(name);
  }
}

import {
  NormalizedErrorStackOptions,
  RedactPaths,
  StripInternalFrames,
} from './error-options.js';

export function normalizeStackNewlines(stack: string): string {
  return stack.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n');
}

const SUPERJSON_INTERNAL_MARKERS = [
  'src/transformer.ts',
  'src/plainer.ts',
  'src/index.ts',
];

function trimLeadingWhitespace(line: string): string {
  return line.replace(/^\s+/, '');
}

function redactPath(line: string, mode: RedactPaths): string {
  if (mode === 'none') {
    return line;
  }

  if (mode === 'basename') {
    // Replace "path/to/file.ts:1:2" with "file.ts:1:2" inside parentheses.
    return line.replace(/\(([^()]*?)([^/\\()]+)\)/g, '($2)');
  }

  // strip_cwd - remove the current working directory prefix.
  const cwd = process.cwd();
  return line.split(cwd).join('');
}

function stripInternalFrames(
  frames: string[],
  mode: StripInternalFrames
): string[] {
  if (mode === 'none') {
    return frames;
  }

  return frames.filter((frame) => {
    if (
      (mode === 'node' || mode === 'node_and_superjson') &&
      frame.includes('node:internal')
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (mode === 'superjson' || mode === 'node_and_superjson') &&
      SUPERJSON_INTERNAL_MARKERS.some((marker) => frame.includes(marker))
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  });
}

function applyMaxStackLines(
  frames: string[],
  maxStackLines: number | undefined
): string[] {
  if (maxStackLines === undefined) {
    return frames;
  }

  // maxStackLines counts the header line, which is kept separately.
  const maxFrames = Math.max(0, maxStackLines - 1);
  return frames.slice(0, maxFrames);
}

export function processStackString(
  stack: string,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions
): string {
  const lines = normalizeStackNewlines(stack).split('\n');
  const header = lines[0] ?? '';
  let frames = lines.slice(1);

  if (options.trimLeadingWhitespace) {
    frames = frames.map(trimLeadingWhitespace);
  }

  if (options.redactPaths !== 'none') {
    frames = frames.map((frame) => redactPath(frame, options.redactPaths));
  }

  frames = applyMaxStackLines(frames, options.maxStackLines);

  frames = stripInternalFrames(frames, options.stripInternalFrames);

  return [header, ...frames].join('\n');
}

export function processStackFrames(
  stack: string,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions
): { raw: string }[] {
  const lines = normalizeStackNewlines(stack).split('\n');
  const header = lines[0] ?? '';
  let frames = lines.slice(1);

  if (options.trimLeadingWhitespace) {
    frames = frames.map(trimLeadingWhitespace);
  }

  frames = stripInternalFrames(frames, options.stripInternalFrames);

  if (options.redactPaths !== 'none') {
    frames = frames.map((frame) => redactPath(frame, options.redactPaths));
  }

  frames = applyMaxStackLines(frames, options.maxStackLines);

  return [{ raw: header }, ...frames.map((frame) => ({ raw: frame }))];
}

import {
  NormalizedErrorStackOptions,
  RedactPaths,
  StripInternalFrames,
} from './error-options.js';

export function normalizeStackNewlines(stack: string): string {
  return stack.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n');
}

const SUPERJSON_INTERNAL_MARKERS = [
  'src/transformer.ts',
  'src/plainer.ts',
  'src/index.ts',
];

function trimLeadingWhitespace(line: string): string {
  return line.replace(/^\s+/, '');
}

function redactPath(line: string, mode: RedactPaths): string {
  if (mode === 'none') {
    return line;
  }

  if (mode === 'basename') {
    // Replace "path/to/file.ts:1:2" with "file.ts:1:2" inside parentheses.
    return line.replace(/\(([^()]*?)([^/\\()]+)\)/g, '($2)');
  }

  // strip_cwd - remove the current working directory prefix.
  const cwd = process.cwd();
  return line.split(cwd).join('');
}

function stripInternalFrames(
  frames: string[],
  mode: StripInternalFrames
): string[] {
  if (mode === 'none') {
    return frames;
  }

  return frames.filter((frame) => {
    if (
      (mode === 'node' || mode === 'node_and_superjson') &&
      frame.includes('node:internal')
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      (mode === 'superjson' || mode === 'node_and_superjson') &&
      SUPERJSON_INTERNAL_MARKERS.some((marker) => frame.includes(marker))
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  });
}

function applyMaxStackLines(
  frames: string[],
  maxStackLines: number | undefined
): string[] {
  if (maxStackLines === undefined) {
    return frames;
  }

  // maxStackLines counts the header line, which is kept separately.
  const maxFrames = Math.max(0, maxStackLines - 1);
  return frames.slice(0, maxFrames);
}

export function processStackString(
  stack: string,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions
): string {
  const lines = normalizeStackNewlines(stack).split('\n');
  const header = lines[0] ?? '';
  let frames = lines.slice(1);

  if (options.trimLeadingWhitespace) {
    frames = frames.map(trimLeadingWhitespace);
  }

  if (options.redactPaths !== 'none') {
    frames = frames.map((frame) => redactPath(frame, options.redactPaths));
  }

  frames = applyMaxStackLines(frames, options.maxStackLines);

  frames = stripInternalFrames(frames, options.stripInternalFrames);

  return [header, ...frames].join('\n');
}

export function processStackFrames(
  stack: string,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions
): { raw: string }[] {
  const lines = normalizeStackNewlines(stack).split('\n');
  const header = lines[0] ?? '';
  let frames = lines.slice(1);

  if (options.trimLeadingWhitespace) {
    frames = frames.map(trimLeadingWhitespace);
  }

  frames = stripInternalFrames(frames, options.stripInternalFrames);

  if (options.redactPaths !== 'none') {
    frames = frames.map((frame) => redactPath(frame, options.redactPaths));
  }

  frames = applyMaxStackLines(frames, options.maxStackLines);

  return [{ raw: header }, ...frames.map((frame) => ({ raw: frame }))];
}

Now helper functions before simpleRules:

```ts
const matchesClassFilter = (
  error: Error,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions | undefined
): boolean => {
  if (!options || options.classFilter.length === 0) {
    return true;
  }
  return options.classFilter.includes(error.name);
};

const isErrorStackString = (
  v: any,
  superJson: SuperJSON
): v is Error =>
  isError(v) &&
  superJson.errorStackOptions?.mode === 'string' &&
  matchesClassFilter(v, superJson.errorStackOptions);

const isErrorStackFrames = (
  v: any,
  superJson: SuperJSON
): v is Error =>
  isError(v) &&
  superJson.errorStackOptions?.mode === 'frames' &&
  matchesClassFilter(v, superJson.errorStackOptions);

const isDefaultError = (
  v: any,
  superJson: SuperJSON
): v is Error =>
  isError(v) &&
  !isErrorStackString(v, superJson) &&
  !isErrorStackFrames(v, superJson);
```

Wait, `matchesClassFilter` returns true when options undefined. But `isErrorStackString` checks mode first, so options must be defined. For
`isDefaultError`, `superJson.errorStackOptions?.mode` — if undefined, not string/frames, so default. Good.

Now serialization helpers:

```ts
const isAggregateError = (error: any): error is any =>
  typeof AggregateError !== 'undefined' && error instanceof AggregateError;

const serializeErrorLeaf = (
  error: Error,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions
): any => {
  const result: any = {
    name: error.name,
    message: error.message,
  };

  if (options.sanitizeMessage) {
    result.message = sanitizeMessage(result.message);
  }

  if (isAggregateError(error) && 'errors' in error) {
    result.errors = (error as any).errors;
  }

  return result;
};
```

Wait, `isAggregateError` — `typeof AggregateError !== 'undefined'` — in a browser without AggregateError, referencing `AggregateError` in `error
instanceof AggregateError` would throw if AggregateError undefined. The guard with `typeof` avoids that. But TypeScript might complain about
referencing AggregateError in a typeof check? It's fine; `typeof AggregateError !== 'undefined'` is safe. Actually the expression `typeof
AggregateError !== 'undefined' && error instanceof AggregateError` — TS evaluates `error instanceof AggregateError` only if typeof check true at
runtime. TS type narrowing: `typeof AggregateError !== 'undefined'` narrows to... hmm, TS may still consider AggregateError possibly undefined for
`instanceof`. Since lib has AggregateError as a value, it's typed as `AggregateErrorConstructor` (not undefined), so fine. Runtime is safe.

Actually, to be extra safe with TS and older environments, I can write:
```ts
const isAggregateError = (error: any): error is any =>
  (error as any)?.constructor?.name === 'AggregateError';
```
That avoids referencing AggregateError entirely. But `error.constructor.name === 'AggregateError'` could false-positive for user classes named
AggregateError. Better to use `instanceof`. Node 16 has AggregateError, so fine. I'll use the typeof guard.

Now `serializeErrorWithOptions`:

```ts
const serializeErrorWithOptions = (
  error: Error,
  superJson: SuperJSON,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions,
  causeDepth = 0
): any => {
  const baseError: any = {
    name: error.name,
    message: error.message,
  };

  if (options.includeCauses !== 'none' && 'cause' in error) {
    const cause = serializeErrorCause(error.cause, options, superJson, causeDepth);
    if (cause !== undefined) {
      baseError.cause = cause;
    }
  }

  if (isAggregateError(error) && 'errors' in error) {
    baseError.errors = (error as any).errors;
  }

  superJson.allowedErrorProps.forEach((prop) => {
    if (prop === 'stack') {
      if (options.mode === 'string' && typeof (error as any).stack === 'string') {
        baseError.stack = processStackString((error as any).stack, options);
      }
    } else if (prop === 'stackFrames') {
      if (options.mode === 'frames' && typeof (error as any).stack === 'string') {
        baseError.stackFrames = processStackFrames((error as any).stack, options);
      } else {
        baseError.stackFrames = (error as any).stackFrames;
      }
    } else {
      baseError[prop] = (error as any)[prop];
    }
  });

  if (options.sanitizeMessage) {
    baseError.message = sanitizeMessage(baseError.message);
  }

  const processor = superJson.errorClassRegistry.getProcessor(error.name);
  if (processor) {
    return processor(baseError);
  }

  return baseError;
};
```

Wait — for deep causes, `serializeErrorWithOptions` is called recursively on each cause, and each applies sanitize and allowed props and processor.
For direct mode, `serializeErrorLeaf` is used for the immediate cause. Good.

`serializeErrorCause`:

```ts
const serializeErrorCause = (
  cause: unknown,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions,
  superJson: SuperJSON,
  depth: number
): any => {
  if (!isError(cause)) {
    return undefined;
  }

  if (options.includeCauses === 'direct') {
    return serializeErrorLeaf(cause, options);
  }

  if (depth >= options.maxCauseDepth) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return serializeErrorWithOptions(cause, superJson, options, depth + 1);
};
```

Wait, the depth check: For deep mode with maxCauseDepth=1:
- top serializeErrorWithOptions(error, depth=0) calls serializeErrorCause(error.cause, depth=0)
- depth 0 >= 1? false -> serializeErrorWithOptions(cause, depth=1)
- that cause's cause: serializeErrorCause(cause2, depth=1) -> 1>=1 true -> undefined. So 1 level of cause kept. Good.

For maxCauseDepth=2:
- level0 cause: depth0 <2 -> serialize cause (depth1)
- cause's cause: depth1 <2 -> serialize (depth2)
- cause2's cause: depth2 >=2 -> stop. So 2 levels. Good.

Now reconstructError:

```ts
const reconstructError = (value: any): Error => {
  if (value instanceof Error) {
    return value;
  }

  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    if (Array.isArray(value.errors)) {
      const e = new AggregateError(value.errors, value.message ?? '');
      e.name = value.name ?? 'Error';
      if (value.stack !== undefined) e.stack = value.stack;
      if (value.cause !== undefined) e.cause = reconstructError(value.cause);
      return e;
    }

    const e = new Error(value.message ?? '');
    e.name = value.name ?? 'Error';
    if (value.stack !== undefined) e.stack = value.stack;
    if (value.cause !== undefined) e.cause = reconstructError(value.cause);
    return e;
  }

  return new Error(String(value));
};
```

Hmm, but `value.cause` could be an Error already (if a processor returned a cause that got annotated). `reconstructError` handles that. `value.errors`
array elements are already Errors (annotated). Good.

Wait, but `AggregateError` constructor signature: `new AggregateError(errors: Iterable<any>, message?: string)`. Good.

But there's an issue: if `value.errors` is present but `value` is not actually an AggregateError (e.g., a processor added errors to a normal error),
reconstructing as AggregateError is fine.

Now the untransform for stack/frames rules:

```ts
const errorStackUntransform = (v: any, superJson: SuperJSON) => {
  const e: any = Array.isArray(v.errors)
    ? new AggregateError(v.errors, v.message ?? '')
    : new Error(v.message ?? '');
  e.name = v.name ?? 'Error';
  if (v.stack !== undefined) e.stack = v.stack;
  if (v.stackFrames !== undefined) e.stackFrames = v.stackFrames;
  if (v.cause !== undefined) e.cause = reconstructError(v.cause);
  if (Array.isArray(v.errors)) {
    e.errors = v.errors;
  }
  superJson.allowedErrorProps.forEach((prop) => {
    if (prop === 'stack' || prop === 'stackFrames') return;
    (e as any)[prop] = v[prop];
  });
  return e;
};
```

Wait — `new AggregateError(v.errors, ...)` then `e.errors = v.errors` redundant but harmless.

But what about `v.errors` when it's an array of Error objects already? Good.

Now the default error untransform stays same.

  v: any,
  superJson: SuperJSON
): v is Error =>
  isError(v) &&
  !isErrorStackString(v, superJson) &&
  !isErrorStackFrames(v, superJson);

const isAggregateError = (error: any): error is any =>
  typeof AggregateError !== 'undefined' && error instanceof AggregateError;

const serializeErrorLeaf = (
  error: Error,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions
): any => {
  const result: any = {
    name: error.name,
    message: error.message,
  };

  if (options.sanitizeMessage) {
    result.message = sanitizeMessage(result.message);
  }

  if (isAggregateError(error) && 'errors' in error) {
    result.errors = (error as any).errors;
  }

  return result;
};

const serializeErrorWithOptions = (
  error: Error,
  superJson: SuperJSON,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions,
  causeDepth = 0
): any => {
  const baseError: any = {
    name: error.name,
    message: error.message,
  };

  if (options.includeCauses !== 'none' && 'cause' in error) {
    const cause = serializeErrorCause(
      error.cause,
      options,
      superJson,
      causeDepth
    );
    if (cause !== undefined) {
      baseError.cause = cause;
    }
  }

  if (isAggregateError(error) && 'errors' in error) {
    baseError.errors = (error as any).errors;
  }

  superJson.allowedErrorProps.forEach(prop => {
    if (prop === 'stack') {
      if (
        options.mode === 'string' &&
        typeof (error as any).stack === 'string'
      ) {
        baseError.stack = processStackString((error as any).stack, options);
      }
    } else if (prop === 'stackFrames') {
      if (
        options.mode === 'frames' &&
        typeof (error as any).stack === 'string'
      ) {
        baseError.stackFrames = processStackFrames(
          (error as any).stack,
          options
        );
      } else {
        baseError.stackFrames = (error as any).stackFrames;
      }
    } else {
      baseError[prop] = (error as any)[prop];
    }
  });

  if (options.sanitizeMessage) {
    baseError.message = sanitizeMessage(baseError.message);
  }

  const processor = superJson.errorClassRegistry.getProcessor(error.name);
  if (processor) {
    return processor(baseError);
  }

  return baseError;
};

const serializeErrorCause = (
  cause: unknown,
  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions,
  superJson: SuperJSON,
  depth: number
): any => {
  if (!isError(cause)) {
    return undefined;
  }

  if (options.includeCauses === 'direct') {
    return serializeErrorLeaf(cause, options);
  }

  if (depth >= options.maxCauseDepth) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return serializeErrorWithOptions(cause, superJson, options, depth + 1);
};

const reconstructError = (value: any): Error => {
  if (value instanceof Error) {
    return value;
  }

  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    const base: any = Array.isArray(value.errors)
      ? new AggregateError(value.errors, value.message ?? '')
      : new Error(value.message ?? '');
    base.name = value.name ?? 'Error';
    if (value.stack !== undefined) {
      base.stack = value.stack;
    }
    if (value.cause !== undefined) {
      base.cause = reconstructError(value.cause);
    }
    return base;
  }

  return new Error(String(value));
};

const errorStackUntransform = (v: any, superJson: SuperJSON) => {
  const e: any = Array.isArray(v.errors)
    ? new AggregateError(v.errors, v.message ?? '')
    : new Error(v.message ?? '');
  e.name = v.name ?? 'Error';
  if (v.stack !== undefined) {
    e.stack = v.stack;
  }
  if (v.stackFrames !== undefined) {
    e.stackFrames = v.stackFrames;
  }
  if (v.cause !== undefined) {
    e.cause = reconstructError(v.cause);
  }
  if (Array.isArray(v.errors)) {
    e.errors = v.errors;
  }

  superJson.allowedErrorProps.forEach(prop => {
    if (prop === 'stack' || prop === 'stackFrames') {
      return;
    }
    (e as any)[prop] = v[prop];
  });

  return e;
};

    82	  superJson: SuperJSON
    83	): v is Error =>
    84	  isError(v) &&
    85	  superJson.errorStackOptions?.mode === 'frames' &&
    86	  matchesClassFilter(v, superJson.errorStackOptions);
    87	
    88	const isDefaultError = (
    89	  v: any,
    90	  superJson: SuperJSON
    91	): v is Error =>
    92	  isError(v) &&
    93	  !isErrorStackString(v, superJson) &&
    94	  !isErrorStackFrames(v, superJson);
    95	
    96	const isAggregateError = (error: any): error is any =>
    97	  typeof AggregateError !== 'undefined' && error instanceof AggregateError;
    98	
    99	const serializeErrorLeaf = (
   100	  error: Error,
   101	  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions
   102	): any => {
   103	  const result: any = {
   104	    name: error.name,
   105	    message: error.message,
   106	  };
   107	
   108	  if (options.sanitizeMessage) {
   109	    result.message = sanitizeMessage(result.message);
   110	  }
   111	
   112	  if (isAggregateError(error) && 'errors' in error) {
   113	    result.errors = (error as any).errors;
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  return result;
   117	};
   118	
   119	const serializeErrorWithOptions = (
   120	  error: Error,
   121	  superJson: SuperJSON,
   122	  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions,
   123	  causeDepth = 0
   124	): any => {
   125	  const baseError: any = {
   126	    name: error.name,
   127	    message: error.message,
   128	  };
   129	
   130	  if (options.includeCauses !== 'none' && 'cause' in error) {
   131	    const cause = serializeErrorCause(
   132	      error.cause,
   133	      options,
   134	      superJson,
   135	      causeDepth
   136	    );
   137	    if (cause !== undefined) {
   138	      baseError.cause = cause;
   139	    }
   140	  }
   141	
   142	  if (isAggregateError(error) && 'errors' in error) {
   143	    baseError.errors = (error as any).errors;
   144	  }
   145	
   146	  superJson.allowedErrorProps.forEach(prop => {
   147	    if (prop === 'stack') {
   148	      if (
   149	        options.mode === 'string' &&
   150	        typeof (error as any).stack === 'string'
   151	      ) {
   152	        baseError.stack = processStackString((error as any).stack, options);
   153	      }
   154	    } else if (prop === 'stackFrames') {
   155	      if (
   156	        options.mode === 'frames' &&
   157	        typeof (error as any).stack === 'string'
   158	      ) {
   159	        baseError.stackFrames = processStackFrames(
   160	          (error as any).stack,
   161	          options
   162	        );
   163	      } else {
   164	        baseError.stackFrames = (error as any).stackFrames;
   165	      }
   166	    } else {
   167	      baseError[prop] = (error as any)[prop];
   168	    }
   169	  });
   170	
   171	  if (options.sanitizeMessage) {
   172	    baseError.message = sanitizeMessage(baseError.message);
   173	  }
   174	
   175	  const processor = superJson.errorClassRegistry.getProcessor(error.name);
   176	  if (processor) {
   177	    return processor(baseError);
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  return baseError;
   181	};
   182	
   183	const serializeErrorCause = (
   184	  cause: unknown,
   185	  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions,
   186	  superJson: SuperJSON,
   187	  depth: number
   188	): any => {
   189	  if (!isError(cause)) {
   190	    return undefined;
   191	  }
   192	
   193	  if (options.includeCauses === 'direct') {
   194	    return serializeErrorLeaf(cause, options);
   195	  }
   196	
   197	  if (depth >= options.maxCauseDepth) {
   198	    return undefined;
   199	  }
   200	
   201	  return serializeErrorWithOptions(cause, superJson, options, depth + 1);
   202	};
   203	
   204	const reconstructError = (value: any): Error => {
   205	  if (value instanceof Error) {
   206	    return value;
   207	  }
   208	
   209	  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
   210	    const base: any = Array.isArray(value.errors)
   211	      ? new AggregateError(value.errors, value.message ?? '')
   212	      : new Error(value.message ?? '');
   213	    base.name = value.name ?? 'Error';
   214	    if (value.stack !== undefined) {
   215	      base.stack = value.stack;
   216	    }
   217	    if (value.cause !== undefined) {
   218	      base.cause = reconstructError(value.cause);
   219	    }
   220	    return base;
   221	  }
   222	
   223	  return new Error(String(value));
   224	};
   225	
   226	const errorStackUntransform = (v: any, superJson: SuperJSON) => {
   227	  const e: any = Array.isArray(v.errors)
   228	    ? new AggregateError(v.errors, v.message ?? '')
   229	    : new Error(v.message ?? '');
   230	  e.name = v.name ?? 'Error';
   231	  if (v.stack !== undefined) {
   232	    e.stack = v.stack;
   233	  }
   234	  if (v.stackFrames !== undefined) {
   235	    e.stackFrames = v.stackFrames;
   236	  }
   237	  if (v.cause !== undefined) {
   238	    e.cause = reconstructError(v.cause);
   239	  }
   240	  if (Array.isArray(v.errors)) {
   241	    e.errors = v.errors;
   242	  }
   243	
   244	  superJson.allowedErrorProps.forEach(prop => {
   245	    if (prop === 'stack' || prop === 'stackFrames') {
   246	      return;
   247	    }
   248	    (e as any)[prop] = v[prop];
   249	  });
   250	
   251	  return e;
   252	};
   253

   255	  simpleTransformation(
   256	    isUndefined,
   257	    'undefined',
   258	    () => null,
   259	    () => undefined
   260	  ),

patched index.ts
import { Class, JSONValue, SuperJSONResult, SuperJSONValue } from './types.js';
import { ClassRegistry, RegisterOptions } from './class-registry.js';
import { Registry } from './registry.js';
import {
  CustomTransfomer,
  CustomTransformerRegistry,
} from './custom-transformer-registry.js';
import {
  ErrorStackOptions,
  NormalizedErrorStackOptions,
  normalizeErrorStackOptions,
} from './error-options.js';
import {
  ErrorClassRegistry,
  ErrorStackProcessor,
} from './error-class-registry.js';
import {
  applyReferentialEqualityAnnotations,
  applyValueAnnotations,
  generateReferentialEqualityAnnotations,
  walker,
} from './plainer.js';
import { copy } from 'copy-anything';

export default class SuperJSON {
  /**
   * If true, SuperJSON will make sure only one instance of referentially equal objects are serialized and the rest are replaced with `null`.
   */
  private readonly dedupe: boolean;

  readonly errorStackOptions: NormalizedErrorStackOptions | undefined;

  /**
   * @param dedupeReferentialEqualities  If true, SuperJSON will make sure only one instance of referentially equal objects are serialized and the rest are replaced with `null`.
   * @param errorStack  Options controlling how Error stack data is serialized.
   */
  constructor({
    dedupe = false,
    errorStack,
  }: {
    dedupe?: boolean;
    errorStack?: ErrorStackOptions;
  } = {}) {
    this.dedupe = dedupe;
    this.errorStackOptions = normalizeErrorStackOptions(errorStack);
  }

  serialize(object: SuperJSONValue): SuperJSONResult {
    const identities = new Map<any, any[][]>();
    const output = walker(object, identities, this, this.dedupe);
    const res: SuperJSONResult = {
      json: output.transformedValue,
    };

    if (output.annotations) {
      res.meta = {
        ...res.meta,
        values: output.annotations,
      };
    }

    const equalityAnnotations = generateReferentialEqualityAnnotations(
      identities,
      this.dedupe
    );
    if (equalityAnnotations) {
      res.meta = {
        ...res.meta,
        referentialEqualities: equalityAnnotations,
      };
    }

    if (res.meta) res.meta.v = 1;

    return res;
  }

  deserialize<T = unknown>(payload: SuperJSONResult, options?: { inPlace?: boolean }): T {
    const { json, meta } = payload;

    let result: T = options?.inPlace ? json : copy(json) as any;

    if (meta?.values) {
      result = applyValueAnnotations(result, meta.values, meta.v ?? 0, this);
    }

    if (meta?.referentialEqualities) {
      result = applyReferentialEqualityAnnotations(
        result,
        meta.referentialEqualities,
        meta.v ?? 0
      );
    }

    return result;
  }

  stringify(object: SuperJSONValue): string {
    return JSON.stringify(this.serialize(object));
  }

  parse<T = unknown>(string: string): T {
    return this.deserialize(JSON.parse(string), { inPlace: true });
  }

  readonly classRegistry = new ClassRegistry();
  registerClass(v: Class, options?: RegisterOptions | string) {
    this.classRegistry.register(v, options);
  }

  readonly symbolRegistry = new Registry<Symbol>(s => s.description ?? '');
  registerSymbol(v: Symbol, identifier?: string) {
    this.symbolRegistry.register(v, identifier);
  }

  readonly customTransformerRegistry = new CustomTransformerRegistry();
  registerCustom<I, O extends JSONValue>(
    transformer: Omit<CustomTransfomer<I, O>, 'name'>,
    name: string
  ) {
    this.customTransformerRegistry.register({
      name,
      ...transformer,
    });
  }

  readonly allowedErrorProps: string[] = [];
  allowErrorProps(...props: string[]) {
    this.allowedErrorProps.push(...props);
  }

  readonly errorClassRegistry = new ErrorClassRegistry();
  registerErrorStackProcessor(className: string, fn: ErrorStackProcessor) {
    this.errorClassRegistry.register(className, fn);
  }

  private static defaultInstance = new SuperJSON();
  static serialize = SuperJSON.defaultInstance.serialize.bind(
    SuperJSON.defaultInstance
  );
  static deserialize = SuperJSON.defaultInstance.deserialize.bind(
    SuperJSON.defaultInstance
  );
  static stringify = SuperJSON.defaultInstance.stringify.bind(
    SuperJSON.defaultInstance
  );
  static parse = SuperJSON.defaultInstance.parse.bind(
    SuperJSON.defaultInstance
  );
  static registerClass = SuperJSON.defaultInstance.registerClass.bind(
    SuperJSON.defaultInstance
  );
  static registerSymbol = SuperJSON.defaultInstance.registerSymbol.bind(
    SuperJSON.defaultInstance
  );
  static registerCustom = SuperJSON.defaultInstance.registerCustom.bind(
    SuperJSON.defaultInstance
  );
  static allowErrorProps = SuperJSON.defaultInstance.allowErrorProps.bind(
    SuperJSON.defaultInstance
  );
  static registerErrorStackProcessor =
    SuperJSON.defaultInstance.registerErrorStackProcessor.bind(
      SuperJSON.defaultInstance
    );
}

export { SuperJSON, SuperJSONResult, SuperJSONValue };

export const serialize = SuperJSON.serialize;
export const deserialize = SuperJSON.deserialize;

export const stringify = SuperJSON.stringify;
export const parse = SuperJSON.parse;

export const registerClass = SuperJSON.registerClass;
export const registerCustom = SuperJSON.registerCustom;
export const registerSymbol = SuperJSON.registerSymbol;
export const allowErrorProps = SuperJSON.allowErrorProps;
export const registerErrorStackProcessor =
  SuperJSON.registerErrorStackProcessor;

1. In `serializeErrorWithOptions`, for direct causes, `serializeErrorLeaf` returns a plain object `{name, message}`. On untransform,

2. But wait — there's a critical issue with how the plainer handles the transformed baseError for Error/stack and Error/frames: The plainer calls
`transformValue` which returns `{ value: baseError, type: 'Error/stack' }`. Then it walks the transformed baseError and recursively transforms each
property. For `baseError.cause` which is a plain object, it will be walked as a plain object (no annotation). For `baseError.errors` array of Error
objects, they get Error annotations. Good.

`v.errors`, it's an array of Error instances. For `v.cause`, it's a plain object (since no annotation). Good.

Let me first think through a couple of subtle issues with my implementation that tests might reveal:

**Issue A: `superJson.errorStackOptions!` non-null in transform when isErrorStackString matches.** Since mode string implies options defined. OK.

**Issue B: default Error rule transform special-cases stack when errorStackOptions defined.** Good.

**Issue C: `processStackFrames` output with `maxStackLines`.** If maxStackLines=1, frames = [] after slice(0,0), output `[{raw: header}]`. Good.

**Issue D: `normalizeStackNewlines` and split.** If stack has CRLF, normalize converts. Good.

**Issue E: classFilter with `Error` class name.** For `new Error('x')`, `error.name` is 'Error'. If classFilter: ['Error'], matches. If classFilter:
['TypeError'] and error is Error, doesn't match -> default rule. Good.

**Issue F: `registerErrorStackProcessor` hook receiving `cause` plain object.** The hook receives baseError before plainer walks. Good.

**Issue G: Frame stack round-trip through container types.** If the hook adds a Map inside stackFrames, the plainer walks it and annotates Map. On
deserialize, v.stackFrames elements are plain objects with `raw` plus maybe map. `errorStackUntransform` sets `e.stackFrames = v.stackFrames` (already
reconstructed). Good.

**Issue H: For Error/stack rule, `v.cause` on untransform.** If includeCauses=none, no cause. If direct, plain object -> reconstruct. If deep, nested
plain objects -> reconstruct recursively. Good.

But wait — there's an issue with deep causes and the plainer. When `serializeErrorWithOptions` returns baseError with a nested `cause` plain object,
the plainer walks it. The plain object has nested `cause` etc. Those are plain objects, so no annotations. Good. But there's another subtlety: the
nested plain cause objects have `name`, `message`, and `cause` fields. The plainer walks them fine. On untransform, reconstructError handles
recursively. Good.

**Issue I: What about the top-level error being AggregateError with mode string?** baseError.errors = error.errors (array of Error). Plainer walks and
annotates each as Error/stack (if they match). On untransform, v.errors is array of Error instances. `errorStackUntransform` creates AggregateError.
Good.

**Issue J: `sanitizeMessage` on cause messages.** In direct mode, `serializeErrorLeaf` sanitizes cause message. In deep mode,
`serializeErrorWithOptions` sanitizes each cause's message. Good.

**Issue K: `includeCauses` with `maxCauseDepth` when includeCauses='direct' and maxCauseDepth invalid.** Normalization: if maxCauseDepth non-integer,
includeCauses becomes none. So direct + invalid maxCauseDepth => none. Per spec: "If maxCauseDepth is present but not an integer, fall back to
includeCauses=none." Yes, applies regardless of includeCauses. Good.

**Issue L: `maxStackLines` invalid => mode off.** Normalization sets mode='off'. But also `maxStackLines` stays undefined in that case. Good.

**Issue M: What if `errorStack` provided with `mode: 'off'` and `includeCauses: 'deep'`?** Since mode=off, default Error rule used, causes still
serialized legacy (raw cause). includeCauses ignored. Is this spec-compliant? The spec says includeCauses defaults none; but in off mode, Error rule
used. The spec doesn't explicitly say includeCauses applies in off mode. Since "Error for off/default/classFilter miss" and the Error rule preserves
existing behavior, I think it's fine. But one could argue that if you set errorStack: {mode:'off', includeCauses:'deep'}, you'd expect deep causes.
However, the mode is off — no stack processing, so likely no cause processing either. I'll keep it.

Hmm, but actually let me reconsider — maybe the intended behavior is that `includeCauses`, `sanitizeMessage` apply regardless of mode, as long as
errorStack is provided? The spec says "classFilter restricts stack processing and sanitization to errors with matching .name". This implies
sanitization is tied to stack processing (which happens in string/frames mode). And "includeCauses" is listed among error stack options. I'll keep the
interpretation that they only apply in string/frames modes.

But wait — there's a subtlety. If errorStack provided with mode='string', classFilter=['TypeError'], and you serialize a plain `Error` (not
TypeError), it uses default Error rule and still serializes causes (legacy). That's consistent with "classFilter miss" using Error rule.

**Issue N: `allowErrorProps('stack')` with mode='string' — the untransform sets e.stack.** Good. `allowErrorProps('stack')` with mode='frames' — stack
not serialized, and untransform won't set stack (since v.stack undefined). Good.

**Issue O: For frames mode, the `stackFrames` array gets walked by plainer.** The plain objects `{ raw: string }` are walked and serialized. On
deserialize they're plain objects. Good.

cd /app && cat > src/tmp-error-stack.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import SuperJSON from './index.js';
import {
  normalizeErrorStackOptions,
} from './error-options.js';
import {
  processStackString,
  processStackFrames,
  normalizeStackNewlines,
} from './error-stack.js';
import { sanitizeMessage } from './error-sanitizer.js';

describe('tmp error stack', () => {
  it('basic string mode', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: {
        mode: 'string',
        maxStackLines: 3,
        stripInternalFrames: 'node',
      },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack = 'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)\n    at b (node:internal/x:1:1)\n    at c (/app/c.ts:3:3)';
    const res = sj.serialize({ e });
    console.log(JSON.stringify(res, null, 2));
    const out: any = sj.deserialize(res);
    console.log('deserialized stack:', JSON.stringify(out.e.stack));
    expect(out.e.stack).toBe('Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)\n    at c (/app/c.ts:3:3)');
  });

  it('frames mode', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: { mode: 'frames', maxStackLines: 3, stripInternalFrames: 'node' },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stackFrames');
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack = 'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)\n    at b (node:internal/x:1:1)\n    at c (/app/c.ts:3:3)';
    const res = sj.serialize({ e });
    console.log(JSON.stringify(res, null, 2));
    const out: any = sj.deserialize(res);
    console.log('deserialized stackFrames:', JSON.stringify(out.e.stackFrames));
    expect(out.e.stackFrames).toEqual([
      { raw: 'Error: hello' },
      { raw: 'at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)' },
      { raw: 'at c (/app/c.ts:3:3)' },
    ]);
  });

  it('off mode never serializes stack even if allowed', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({ errorStack: { mode: 'off' } });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack = 'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)';
    const res = sj.serialize({ e });
    const out: any = sj.deserialize(res);
    expect(out.e.stack).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('normalizeErrorStackOptions', () => {
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions(undefined)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions(null)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions('x')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({})!.mode).toBe('off');
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({ mode: 'bogus' })!.mode).toBe('off');
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({ mode: 'string', maxStackLines: 0 })!.mode).toBe('off');
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({ mode: 'string', maxStackLines: 1.5 })!.mode).toBe('off');
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({ includeCauses: 'deep', maxCauseDepth: 1.5 })!.includeCauses).toBe('none');
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({ stripInternalFrames: 'bogus' })!.stripInternalFrames).toBe('none');
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({ redactPaths: 'bogus' })!.redactPaths).toBe('none');
  });

  it('sanitizeMessage', () => {
    expect(sanitizeMessage('go to https://example.com/a?b=1 ok')).toBe('go to [redacted] ok');
    expect(sanitizeMessage('mail me at a.b@example.co.uk please')).toBe('mail me at [redacted] please');
    expect(sanitizeMessage('ip 192.168.1.1 here')).toBe('ip [redacted] here');
  });

  it('process functions', () => {
    const opts = normalizeErrorStackOptions({ mode: 'string', maxStackLines: 2 })!;
    const stack = 'Error: x\r\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)\r\n    at b (/app/b.ts:2:2)\r\n    at c (/app/c.ts:3:3)';
    expect(normalizeStackNewlines(stack)).toBe('Error: x\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)\n    at b (/app/b.ts:2:2)\n    at c (/app/c.ts:3:3)');
    expect(processStackString(stack, opts)).toBe('Error: x\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)');
    expect(processStackFrames(stack, opts)).toEqual([
      { raw: 'Error: x' },
      { raw: 'at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)' },
    ]);
  });
});
EOF

STEP 28 SOURCE agent
REASONING_CONTENT
Interesting — in the basic string mode test, maxStackLines=3 should keep header + 2 frames (a and c). But the output only kept header + 1 frame (a).
Let me look at why.

The stack is:
```
Error: hello
    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)
    at b (node:internal/x:1:1)
    at c (/app/c.ts:3:3)
```

Order in string mode: normalize -> trim -> redact -> maxStackLines -> stripInternalFrames. With maxStackLines=3, after trim we have frames [a, b, c].
applyMaxStackLines keeps first 2 => [a, b]. Then stripInternalFrames removes b (node:internal) => [a]. So output is header + a. That's correct per the
specified order! maxStackLines happens BEFORE stripInternalFrames in string mode. So my test expectation was wrong again — the spec says string-mode
order: normalizeNewlines -> trimLeadingWhitespace -> redactPaths -> maxStackLines -> stripInternalFrames. So indeed truncation before stripping. This
is correct behavior.

In frames mode, order is normalize -> trim -> stripInternalFrames -> redactPaths -> maxStackLines. So strip happens before truncation, meaning b would
be removed first, then truncate keeps [a, c]... but wait, with maxStackLines=3, after stripping b, frames = [a, c], then truncate keeps [a, c]. So
frames mode keeps a and c. That matches my earlier frames test which passed.

So the string mode behavior is per spec. My test expectation should be `Error: hello\nat a (...)` only. Let me fix. This is a good demonstration of
the order difference.

Let me update the temp test.
MESSAGE
This confirms the spec's string-mode order (maxStackLines before stripInternalFrames). My expectation was wrong. Let me fix it.

REASONING_CONTENT
Basic behavior works. Now let me write a comprehensive test suite and add it as `src/error-stack.test.ts` (permanent). I'll cover all the spec
requirements. Then run it and iterate.

Let me think about edge cases to test and potential bugs:

1. `normalizeErrorStackOptions`:
   - undefined/null/string/array => undefined
   - mode invalid/missing => off
   - maxStackLines invalid => mode off
   - maxCauseDepth non-integer => includeCauses none
   - unknown strip/redact => none
   - defaults: trimLeadingWhitespace true, normalizeNewlines false, sanitize false, includeCauses none, maxCauseDepth 16, classFilter []

2. `processStackString`:
   - normalize CRLF/CR
   - trim leading whitespace (true/false)
   - redact basename / strip_cwd
   - maxStackLines counts header
   - stripInternalFrames node/superjson/node_and_superjson
   - order (string: max before strip; frames: strip before max)
   - unknown strip/redact fall back to none (normalization)

3. `processStackFrames`:
   - header as first raw
   - order

4. `sanitizeMessage`: URLs, emails, IPv4

5. `ErrorClassRegistry`: register/has/getProcessor

6. Integration:
   - default (no errorStack) unchanged: no stack unless allowed; raw stack if allowed
   - mode off: no stack even if allowed
   - mode string: processed stack serialized + restored
   - mode frames: stackFrames serialized + restored
   - classFilter: matching processes, non-matching default
   - includeCauses direct/deep/maxCauseDepth/non-Error dropped/circular
   - AggregateError errors round-trip
   - sanitizeMessage applies to own + causes
   - registerErrorStackProcessor hook
   - frames round-trip through container types (via hook adding a Date or Map to stackFrames? Actually the spec says "round-trip through all
SuperJSON-supported container types" — let me test that stackFrames array itself, being a plain array, round-trips. I can test by using a processor
hook to add a Date inside a frame, e.g. `{ raw: 'x', date: new Date() }` and verify it round-trips to Date.)

Let me also think about potential bugs in my implementation that tests might reveal:

**Bug 1: `processStackFrames` when `trimLeadingWhitespace=false` and header.**
With trim false, frames preserve leading whitespace. `{raw: '    at a (...)'}`. Good.

**Bug 2: `redactPath` basename regex may not work on Windows paths, but on Linux fine.**

**Bug 3: `stripInternalFrames` for frames mode — `src/transformer.ts` marker.** In production, compiled code is in dist, so frames may contain
`src/transformer.ts` only when running from source. The spec says strip frames containing those source markers, so fine.

**Bug 4: `includeCauses` deep with maxCauseDepth default 16.** Let me test.

**Bug 5: Circular cause chain in deep mode.** Should terminate at maxCauseDepth. Good.

**Bug 6: `sanitizeMessage` with a URL containing an IP address.** URL regex matches first, so entire URL replaced. Good.

**Bug 7: `registerErrorStackProcessor` hook returning object with a `Map` in stackFrames.** The plainer walks it and annotates Map. Let me verify this
works. The hook runs before plainer walks baseError, so the Map object is inside baseError.stackFrames; plainer walks it. Good.

**Bug 8: When the hook returns a replacement object, does the annotation still apply?** Yes — the rule's transform returns processor(baseError), and
transformValue returns `{value: that, type: 'Error/stack'}`. Good.

**Bug 9: What about `errorStackUntransform` and allowed props 'stack'/'stackFrames' — it skips them, so if v.stack is undefined (e.g. in frames mode
but user allowed stack and frames mode didn't serialize stack), the generic loop skips 'stack' anyway. Fine. But what if in frames mode user also
allowed 'stack'? v.stack undefined, skip. Good.

**Bug 10: `errorStackUntransform` for Error/stack when v.cause is already an Error (could happen if user processor returns a raw Error cause, or if...
actually no, our transform creates plain causes; but a processor could return a raw Error in cause). reconstructError handles `value instanceof
Error`. Good.

**Bug 11: The default Error rule untransform uses `new Error(v.message, { cause: v.cause })` — when v.cause is undefined, fine. When v.cause is a
plain object from legacy? Existing behavior. Good.

**Bug 12: `simpleRulesByAnnotation`** — since I added annotations 'Error/stack' and 'Error/frames', they'll be in the map. Good.

**Bug 13: `serializeErrorWithOptions` is called recursively for deep causes with `causeDepth` increment. But the depth check happens in
`serializeErrorCause`, not at the top of `serializeErrorWithOptions`. Let me trace maxCauseDepth=1:
- serializeErrorWithOptions(top, depth=0)
  - serializeErrorCause(top.cause, depth=0): depth 0 >= 1? false -> serializeErrorWithOptions(cause1, depth=1)
    - serializeErrorCause(cause1.cause, depth=1): 1 >= 1 true -> undefined
  - so cause1 included with no further cause. Good.

**Bug 14: direct mode with `cause` being an AggregateError** — serializeErrorLeaf includes errors. Good.

**Bug 15: In `serializeErrorWithOptions`, `superJson.allowedErrorProps.forEach` — in deep mode recursive calls also iterate allowed props, meaning
causes get stack processed and allowed props copied too. Is that desired? Possibly over-processing but not harmful. Actually, if includeCauses=deep
and mode=string, each cause gets its own stack serialized too. The spec doesn't forbid it. It might even be desirable. But it could cause the causes'
stack to be processed and included in the plain cause object, and then reconstructError restores stack. That's fine.

Hmm wait — but there's a subtle problem: deep causes are plain objects (not Error-annotated), so on untransform reconstructError restores them. If a
cause has `stack`, reconstructError sets it. Good.

**Bug 16: `isAggregateError` references AggregateError in instanceof; on Node 16 it's defined. Good.**

**Bug 17: The `errorStackOptions` field is public; tests might access it. Fine.**

**Bug 18: Static `registerErrorStackProcessor` wrapper is bound after `defaultInstance` is created. Since static field initializers run in order, and
`defaultInstance` is declared before `registerErrorStackProcessor` wrapper, it's fine. Let me verify: In index.ts, `private static defaultInstance =
new SuperJSON();` appears before `static registerErrorStackProcessor`. Good.**

**Bug 19: When `errorStack` is provided with mode='string' and classFilter empty, `matchesClassFilter` returns true. Good.**

**Bug 20: When `errorStack` is provided but mode invalid, `normalizeErrorStackOptions` returns object with mode='off'. Then default Error rule used;
`superJson.errorStackOptions` truthy, so stack is suppressed even if allowed. Good — "if errorStack provided but mode missing/invalid, treat like
mode=off" and off never serializes stack.**

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import SuperJSON from './index.js';
import {
  ErrorStackOptions,
  normalizeErrorStackOptions,
} from './error-options.js';
import {
  normalizeStackNewlines,
  processStackFrames,
  processStackString,
} from './error-stack.js';
import { sanitizeMessage } from './error-sanitizer.js';
import { ErrorClassRegistry } from './error-class-registry.js';

const normalize = (options: ErrorStackOptions) =>
  normalizeErrorStackOptions(options)!;

describe('normalizeErrorStackOptions', () => {
  it('returns undefined for non-object input', () => {
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions(undefined)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions(null)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions('string')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions(42)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions([])).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('treats missing or invalid mode as off', () => {
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({})!.mode).toBe('off');
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({ mode: 'bogus' })!.mode).toBe('off');
    expect(normalizeErrorStackOptions({ mode: 1 })!.mode).toBe('off');
  });

  it('applies defaults', () => {
    const options = normalize({ mode: 'string' });
    expect(options.mode).toBe('string');
    expect(options.normalizeNewlines).toBe(false);
    expect(options.trimLeadingWhitespace).toBe(true);
    expect(options.maxStackLines).toBeUndefined();
    expect(options.stripInternalFrames).toBe('none');
    expect(options.redactPaths).toBe('none');
    expect(options.includeCauses).toBe('none');
    expect(options.maxCauseDepth).toBe(16);
    expect(options.sanitizeMessage).toBe(false);
    expect(options.classFilter).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('falls back to off for invalid maxStackLines', () => {
    expect(normalize({ mode: 'string', maxStackLines: 0 })!.mode).toBe('off');
    expect(normalize({ mode: 'string', maxStackLines: -3 })!.mode).toBe('off');
    expect(normalize({ mode: 'string', maxStackLines: 2.5 })!.mode).toBe(
      'off'
    );
    expect(normalize({ mode: 'string', maxStackLines: '5' })!.mode).toBe('off');
  });

  it('falls back to includeCauses=none for invalid maxCauseDepth', () => {
    expect(
      normalize({ includeCauses: 'deep', maxCauseDepth: 1.5 })!.includeCauses
    ).toBe('none');
    expect(
      normalize({ includeCauses: 'deep', maxCauseDepth: '4' })!.includeCauses
    ).toBe('none');
    expect(normalize({ includeCauses: 'deep' })!.maxCauseDepth).toBe(16);
  });

  it('falls back to none for unknown strip/redact values', () => {
    expect(normalize({ stripInternalFrames: 'bogus' })!.stripInternalFrames).toBe(
      'none'
    );
    expect(normalize({ redactPaths: 'bogus' })!.redactPaths).toBe('none');
  });

  it('normalizes classFilter', () => {
    expect(normalize({ classFilter: 'TypeError' })!.classFilter).toEqual([
      'TypeError',
    ]);
    expect(
      normalize({ classFilter: ['TypeError', 'Error'] })!.classFilter
    ).toEqual(['TypeError', 'Error']);
    expect(normalize({ classFilter: [] })!.classFilter).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('error-stack processing', () => {
  const stack =
    'Error: boom\r\n' +
    '    at alpha (/app/src/alpha.ts:1:1)\r\n' +
    '    at beta (node:internal/modules/run_main.js:1:1)\r\n' +
    '    at gamma (/app/src/transformer.ts:2:2)\r\n' +
    '    at delta (/app/src/delta.ts:3:3)';

  it('normalizeStackNewlines converts CRLF and CR to LF', () => {
    expect(normalizeStackNewlines('a\r\nb\rc')).toBe('a\nb\nc');
  });

  it('trims leading whitespace by default, preserves it when disabled', () => {
    const options = normalize({ mode: 'string' });
    expect(processStackString(stack, options)).toBe(
      'Error: boom\n' +
        'at alpha (/app/src/alpha.ts:1:1)\n' +
        'at beta (node:internal/modules/run_main.js:1:1)\n' +
        'at gamma (/app/src/transformer.ts:2:2)\n' +
        'at delta (/app/src/delta.ts:3:3)'
    );

    const untrimmed = normalize({ mode: 'string', trimLeadingWhitespace: false });
    expect(processStackString(stack, untrimmed)).toBe(
      'Error: boom\n' +
        '    at alpha (/app/src/alpha.ts:1:1)\n' +
        '    at beta (node:internal/modules/run_main.js:1:1)\n' +
        '    at gamma (/app/src/transformer.ts:2:2)\n' +
        '    at delta (/app/src/delta.ts:3:3)'
    );
  });

  it('maxStackLines counts the header line', () => {
    const options = normalize({ mode: 'string', maxStackLines: 2 });
    expect(processStackString(stack, options)).toBe(
      'Error: boom\nat alpha (/app/src/alpha.ts:1:1)'
    );
  });

  it('string mode applies maxStackLines before stripInternalFrames', () => {
    const options = normalize({
      mode: 'string',
      maxStackLines: 3,
      stripInternalFrames: 'node',
    });
    expect(processStackString(stack, options)).toBe(
      'Error: boom\nat alpha (/app/src/alpha.ts:1:1)'
    );
  });

  it('frames mode applies stripInternalFrames before maxStackLines', () => {
    const options = normalize({
      mode: 'frames',
      maxStackLines: 3,
      stripInternalFrames: 'node',
    });
    expect(processStackFrames(stack, options)).toEqual([
      { raw: 'Error: boom' },
      { raw: 'at alpha (/app/src/alpha.ts:1:1)' },
      { raw: 'at gamma (/app/src/transformer.ts:2:2)' },
    ]);
  });

  it('strips node internal frames', () => {
    const options = normalize({ mode: 'string', stripInternalFrames: 'node' });
    const output = processStackString(stack, options);
    expect(output).not.toContain('node:internal');
    expect(output).toContain('at alpha');
  });

  it('strips superjson internal frames', () => {
    const options = normalize({
      mode: 'string',
      stripInternalFrames: 'superjson',
    });
    const output = processStackString(stack, options);
    expect(output).not.toContain('src/transformer.ts');
    expect(output).toContain('at alpha');
    expect(output).toContain('at beta');
  });

  it('strips both node and superjson frames', () => {
    const options = normalize({
      mode: 'string',
      stripInternalFrames: 'node_and_superjson',
    });
    const output = processStackString(stack, options);
    expect(output).not.toContain('node:internal');
    expect(output).not.toContain('src/transformer.ts');
    expect(output).toContain('at delta');
  });

  it('never removes the header line', () => {
    const options = normalize({
      mode: 'string',
      stripInternalFrames: 'node_and_superjson',
      maxStackLines: 1,
    });
    expect(processStackString(stack, options)).toBe('Error: boom');
    expect(processStackFrames(stack, options)).toEqual([{ raw: 'Error: boom' }]);
  });

  it('redacts paths to basenames', () => {
    const options = normalize({ mode: 'string', redactPaths: 'basename' });
    expect(processStackString(stack, options)).toBe(
      'Error: boom\n' +
        'at alpha (alpha.ts:1:1)\n' +
        'at beta (run_main.js:1:1)\n' +
        'at gamma (transformer.ts:2:2)\n' +
        'at delta (delta.ts:3:3)'
    );
  });

  it('removes the cwd prefix', () => {
    const options = normalize({ mode: 'string', redactPaths: 'strip_cwd' });
    const output = processStackString(stack, options);
    expect(output).not.toContain(process.cwd());
    expect(output).toContain('at alpha (/src/alpha.ts:1:1)');
  });

  it('frames output starts with the header as the first raw entry', () => {
    const options = normalize({ mode: 'frames' });
    const frames = processStackFrames(stack, options);
    expect(frames[0]).toEqual({ raw: 'Error: boom' });
    expect(frames.length).toBe(5);
  });
});

describe('sanitizeMessage', () => {
  it('redacts URLs, emails, and IPv4 addresses', () => {
    expect(
      sanitizeMessage('see https://example.com/path?q=1 and http://a.b')
    ).toBe('see [redacted] and [redacted]');
    expect(sanitizeMessage('contact john.doe@example.co.uk now')).toBe(
      'contact [redacted] now'
    );
    expect(sanitizeMessage('server at 192.168.1.1 is down')).toBe(
      'server at [redacted] is down'
    );
  });
});

describe('ErrorClassRegistry', () => {
  it('registers, checks and retrieves processors', () => {
    const registry = new ErrorClassRegistry();
    const fn = () => ({});
    expect(registry.has('Error')).toBe(false);
    expect(registry.getProcessor('Error')).toBeUndefined();
    registry.register('Error', fn);
    expect(registry.has('Error')).toBe(true);
    expect(registry.getProcessor('Error')).toBe(fn);
  });
});

describe('SuperJSON error stack integration', () => {
  it('keeps existing behavior when errorStack is omitted', () => {
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack = 'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)';

    const withoutAllow = SuperJSON.parse(SuperJSON.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(withoutAllow.stack).toBeUndefined();

    const sj = new SuperJSON();
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const withAllow = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(withAllow.stack).toBe(e.stack);
  });

  it('off mode never serializes stack even when allowed', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({ errorStack: { mode: 'off' } });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack = 'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)';
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.stack).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('string mode serializes and restores a processed stack', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: {
        mode: 'string',
        maxStackLines: 2,
        trimLeadingWhitespace: false,
      },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack =
      'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)\n    at b (/app/b.ts:2:2)';

    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.stack).toBe('Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)');
  });

  it('frames mode serializes and restores stackFrames', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({ errorStack: { mode: 'frames' } });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stackFrames');
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack = 'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)\n    at b (/app/b.ts:2:2)';

    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.stackFrames).toEqual([
      { raw: 'Error: hello' },
      { raw: 'at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)' },
      { raw: 'at b (/app/b.ts:2:2)' },
    ]);
  });

  it('classFilter restricts processing to matching error names', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: { mode: 'string', classFilter: ['TypeError'] },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const plain: any = new Error('plain');
    plain.stack = 'Error: plain\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)';
    const type: any = new TypeError('type');
    type.stack = 'TypeError: type\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)';

    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify({ plain, type })) as any;
    expect(out.plain.stack).toBeUndefined();
    expect(out.type.stack).toBe('TypeError: type\nat a (/app/a.ts:1:1)');
  });

  it('empty classFilter means all errors', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: { mode: 'string', classFilter: [] },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack = 'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)';
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.stack).toBe('Error: hello\nat a (/app/a.ts:1:1)');
  });

  it('includeCauses direct keeps the immediate cause', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: { mode: 'string', includeCauses: 'direct' },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e = new Error('outer', { cause: new Error('inner') });
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.cause).toBeInstanceOf(Error);
    expect(out.cause.message).toBe('inner');
    expect('cause' in out.cause).toBe(false);
  });

  it('includeCauses deep keeps causes up to maxCauseDepth', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: {
        mode: 'string',
        includeCauses: 'deep',
        maxCauseDepth: 2,
      },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e = new Error('1', { cause: new Error('2', { cause: new Error('3') }) });
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.cause.message).toBe('2');
    expect(out.cause.cause.message).toBe('3');
    expect('cause' in out.cause.cause).toBe(false);
  });

  it('drops non-Error causes', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: { mode: 'string', includeCauses: 'deep' },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e: any = new Error('outer');
    e.cause = 'not an error';
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.cause).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('stops cleanly on circular cause chains', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: { mode: 'string', includeCauses: 'deep', maxCauseDepth: 3 },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e: any = new Error('outer');
    e.cause = e;
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.cause).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('sanitizes the error message and every kept cause message', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: {
        mode: 'string',
        sanitizeMessage: true,
        includeCauses: 'deep',
        maxCauseDepth: 2,
      },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e = new Error('visit https://example.com', {
      cause: new Error('mail a@b.com'),
    });
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.message).toBe('visit [redacted]');
    expect(out.cause.message).toBe('mail [redacted]');
  });

  it('serializes AggregateError errors as-is and restores them', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({ errorStack: { mode: 'string' } });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    const e = new AggregateError([new Error('one'), new TypeError('two')], 'agg');
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.name).toBe('AggregateError');
    expect(out.message).toBe('agg');
    expect(out.errors).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(out.errors[0]).toBeInstanceOf(Error);
    expect(out.errors[0].message).toBe('one');
    expect(out.errors[1].name).toBe('TypeError');
  });

  it('runs registerErrorStackProcessor after all serialization steps', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: { mode: 'string', sanitizeMessage: true, maxStackLines: 2 },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    sj.registerErrorStackProcessor('Error', (serialized) => {
      expect(serialized.message).toBe('visit [redacted]');
      expect(serialized.stack).toContain('Error: visit [redacted]');
      return { ...serialized, extra: 'added' };
    });
    const e: any = new Error('visit https://example.com');
    e.stack = 'Error: visit https://example.com\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)\n    at b (/app/b.ts:2:2)';
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.extra).toBe('added');
    expect(out.message).toBe('visit [redacted]');
  });

  it('does not run the processor for non-matching classes', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({
      errorStack: { mode: 'string', classFilter: ['TypeError'] },
    });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stack');
    let calls = 0;
    sj.registerErrorStackProcessor('Error', () => {
      calls++;
      return {};
    });
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack = 'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)';
    sj.serialize(e);
    expect(calls).toBe(0);
  });

  it('round-trips container types inside stackFrames', () => {
    const sj = new SuperJSON({ errorStack: { mode: 'frames' } });
    sj.allowErrorProps('stackFrames');
    sj.registerErrorStackProcessor('Error', (serialized) => ({
      ...serialized,
      stackFrames: [
        { raw: 'Error: hello', date: new Date(2020, 1, 1) },
        ...serialized.stackFrames.slice(1),
      ],
    }));
    const e: any = new Error('hello');
    e.stack = 'Error: hello\n    at a (/app/a.ts:1:1)';
    const out = sj.parse(sj.stringify(e)) as any;
    expect(out.stackFrames[0].date).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    expect(out.stackFrames[0].date.getTime()).toBe(new Date(2020, 1, 1).getTime());
  });
});

```ts
  const e: any = Array.isArray(v.errors) ? new AggregateError(...) : new Error(...);
  e.name = v.name ?? 'Error';
  if (v.stack !== undefined) e.stack = v.stack;
  if (v.stackFrames !== undefined) e.stackFrames = v.stackFrames;

};
```

### Re-read spec carefully

1. "Add a new `errorStack` constructor option to SuperJSON. Omitting it leaves existing Error behavior unchanged." ✓
2. "The option shape is `{ mode?, normalizeNewlines?, trimLeadingWhitespace?, maxStackLines?, stripInternalFrames?, redactPaths?, includeCauses?,
maxCauseDepth?, sanitizeMessage?, classFilter? }`. Normalize once at construction time." ✓
3. "Modes are `off`, `string`, and `frames`. `off` never serializes stack data, even if `allowErrorProps` includes `stack`. `string` serializes a
processed stack string when `stack` is allowed. `frames` serializes `stackFrames` as an array of `{ raw: string }` objects when `stackFrames` is
allowed. If `errorStack` is provided but `mode` is missing or invalid, treat it like `mode=off`." ✓
4. "Add three Error rules with annotations `Error`, `Error/stack`, and `Error/frames`. Use `Error` for off/default/classFilter miss, `Error/stack` for
string mode with a matching class name, and `Error/frames` for frames mode with a matching class name." ✓
5. "String-mode order: `normalizeNewlines -> trimLeadingWhitespace -> redactPaths -> maxStackLines -> stripInternalFrames`. Frames-mode order:
`normalizeNewlines -> trimLeadingWhitespace -> stripInternalFrames -> redactPaths -> maxStackLines`." ✓

    63	function applyMaxStackLines(
    64	  frames: string[],
    65	  maxStackLines: number | undefined
    66	): string[] {
    67	  if (maxStackLines === undefined) {
    68	    return frames;
    69	  }
    70	
    71	  // maxStackLines counts the header line, which is kept separately.
    72	  const maxFrames = Math.max(0, maxStackLines - 1);
    73	  return frames.slice(0, maxFrames);
    74	}
    75	
    76	export function processStackString(
    77	  stack: string,
    78	  options: NormalizedErrorStackOptions
    79	): string {
    80	  const normalized = options.normalizeNewlines
    81	    ? normalizeStackNewlines(stack)
    82	    : stack;
    83	  const lines = normalized.split('\n');
    84	  const header = lines[0] ?? '';
    85	  let frames = lines.slice(1);
    86	
    87	  if (options.trimLeadingWhitespace) {
    88	    frames = frames.map(trimLeadingWhitespace);
    89	  }
    90	
    91	  if (options.redactPaths !== 'none') {
    92	    frames = frames.map((frame) => redactPath(frame, options.redactPaths));
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  frames = applyMaxStackLines(frames, options.maxStackLines);
    96	
    97	  frames = stripInternalFrames(frames, options.stripInternalFrames);
    98	
    99	  return [header, ...frames].join('\n');
   100	}
   101

   108	    : stack;
   109	  const lines = normalized.split('\n');
   110	  const header = lines[0] ?? '';
   111	  let frames = lines.slice(1);
   112

CALL s39_c0 TOOL_ID call_00_jd1n4R2cIueEoIQhYjMD4639
FUNCTION bash
ARGUMENTS

path = 'src/error-stack.ts'
with open(path) as f:
    src = f.read()

old = """function applyMaxStackLines(
  frames: string[],
  maxStackLines: number | undefined
): string[] {
  if (maxStackLines === undefined) {
    return frames;
  }

  // maxStackLines counts the header line, which is kept separately.
  const maxFrames = Math.max(0, maxStackLines - 1);
  return frames.slice(0, maxFrames);
}
"""
new = """function applyMaxStackLines(
  frames: string[],
  maxStackLines: number | undefined
): string[] {
  if (maxStackLines === undefined) {
    return frames;
  }

  // maxStackLines counts the header line, which is kept separately.
  const maxFrames = Math.max(0, maxStackLines - 1);
  return frames.slice(0, maxFrames);
}

function splitStack(stack: string, normalizeNewlines: boolean): string[] {
  const normalized = normalizeNewlines ? normalizeStackNewlines(stack) : stack;
  const lines = normalized.split('\\n');

  // Drop trailing empty lines so a trailing CRLF/CR/LF does not create an
  // artificial empty frame.
  while (lines.length > 1 && lines[lines.length - 1] === '') {
    lines.pop();
  }

  return lines;
}
"""
assert old in src
src = src.replace(old, new)

old2 = """  const normalized = options.normalizeNewlines
    ? normalizeStackNewlines(stack)
    : stack;
  const lines = normalized.split('\\n');
  const header = lines[0] ?? '';
  let frames = lines.slice(1);

  if (options.trimLeadingWhitespace) {
    frames = frames.map(trimLeadingWhitespace);
  }

  if (options.redactPaths !== 'none') {
    frames = frames.map((frame) => redactPath(frame, options.redactPaths));
  }

  frames = applyMaxStackLines(frames, options.maxStackLines);

  frames = stripInternalFrames(frames, options.stripInternalFrames);

  return [header, ...frames].join('\\n');"""

  frames = stripInternalFrames(frames, options.stripInternalFrames);

stackFrames, my implementation would serialize stackFrames as-is (because default rule copies it). Hmm.

Let me think about the phrase "stack data" — stackFrames is stack data (it's derived from stack). The mention "even if allowErrorProps includes stack"
is just an example of the strongest case. I think to be safe, off mode should suppress BOTH `stack` and `stackFrames` when errorStackOptions exists.

defined. This is more aligned with "off never serializes stack data".

means "stack processing" doesn't happen for that error, so it's like off for that error. So suppressing both in default rule when errorStackOptions
exists is correct.

```ts

  if (superJson.errorStackOptions && (prop === 'stack' || prop === 'stackFrames')) {
    return;
  }
```

This means when errorStack omitted, existing behavior: stackFrames (if allowed) is copied. When errorStack provided, both stack and stackFrames are
suppressed in default rule. Good.

stackFrames. If user allows stackFrames in string mode, should it be copied? The spec says frames mode serializes stackFrames when allowed. I think in
string mode, stackFrames should NOT be serialized (since stackFrames serialization is a frames-mode thing). Let me update the processing rule: in
string mode, if prop === 'stackFrames', skip it entirely (don't copy as-is). In frames mode, if prop === 'stack', skip it entirely (don't copy as-is).
In off mode (not processing rule), both suppressed. This gives a clean mapping:
- string mode serializes `stack` (processed) if allowed; does NOT serialize `stackFrames`.
- frames mode serializes `stackFrames` (processed) if allowed; does NOT serialize `stack`.
- off/default serializes neither stack nor stackFrames (if errorStack provided).

```ts
    baseError.stack = processStackString(...);

    baseError.stackFrames = processStackFrames(...);
  }
  // else skip

}
```

This is cleaner. Let me update both.

    51	      frame.includes('node:internal')
    52	    ) {
    53	      return false;
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    if (
    57	      (mode === 'superjson' || mode === 'node_and_superjson') &&
    58	      SUPERJSON_INTERNAL_MARKERS.some((marker) => frame.includes(marker))
    59	    ) {
    60	      return false;
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    return true;
    64	  });
    65	}
    66	
    67	function applyMaxStackLines(
    68	  frames: string[],
    69	  maxStackLines: number | undefined
    70	): string[] {
    71	  if (maxStackLines === undefined) {
    72	    return frames;
    73	  }
    74	
    75	  // maxStackLines counts the header line, which is kept separately.
    76	  const maxFrames = Math.max(0, maxStackLines - 1);
    77	  return frames.slice(0, maxFrames);
    78	}
    79	
    80	function splitStack(stack: string, normalizeNewlines: boolean): string[] {
    81	  const normalized = normalizeNewlines ? normalizeStackNewlines(stack) : stack;
    82	  const lines = normalized.split('\n');
    83	
    84	  // Drop trailing empty lines so a trailing CRLF/CR/LF does not create an
    85	  // artificial empty frame.
    86	  while (lines.length > 1 && lines[lines.length - 1] === '') {
    87	    lines.pop();
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  return lines;
    91	}
    92	
    93	export function processStackString(
    94	  stack: string,
    95	  options: ErrorStackOptions
    96	): string {
    97	  const normalizedOptions =
    98	    normalizeErrorStackOptions(options) ?? defaultOptions;
    99	  const lines = splitStack(stack, normalizedOptions.normalizeNewlines);
   100	  const header = lines[0] ?? '';
   101	  let frames = lines.slice(1);
   102	
   103	  if (normalizedOptions.trimLeadingWhitespace) {
   104	    frames = frames.map(trimLeadingWhitespace);
   105	  }
   106	
   107	  if (normalizedOptions.redactPaths !== 'none') {
   108	    frames = frames.map((frame) => redactPath(frame, normalizedOptions.redactPaths));
   109	  }
   110	
   111	  frames = applyMaxStackLines(frames, normalizedOptions.maxStackLines);
   112	
   113	  frames = stripInternalFrames(frames, normalizedOptions.stripInternalFrames);
   114	
   115	  return [header, ...frames].join('\n');
   116	}
   117	
   118	export function processStackFrames(
   119	  stack: string,
   120	  options: ErrorStackOptions
   121	): { raw: string }[] {
   122	  const normalizedOptions =
   123	    normalizeErrorStackOptions(options) ?? defaultOptions;
   124	  const lines = splitStack(stack, normalizedOptions.normalizeNewlines);
   125	  const header = lines[0] ?? '';
   126	  let frames = lines.slice(1);
   127	
   128	  if (normalizedOptions.trimLeadingWhitespace) {
   129	    frames = frames.map(trimLeadingWhitespace);
   130	  }
   131	
   132	  frames = stripInternalFrames(frames, normalizedOptions.stripInternalFrames);
   133	
   134	  if (normalizedOptions.redactPaths !== 'none') {
   135	    frames = frames.map((frame) => redactPath(frame, normalizedOptions.redactPaths));
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  frames = applyMaxStackLines(frames, normalizedOptions.maxStackLines);
   139	
   140	  return [{ raw: header }, ...frames.map((frame) => ({ raw: frame }))];
   141	}
